## Clean-room recommendation
- Create the clean repo from committed `HEAD` using `git archive HEAD`, not from the dirty working tree.
- Do not carry over `audit_outputs`, `artifacts`, `.venv`, `node_modules`, caches, or old screenshots.
- Preserve `data-ff-*` hooks as product contracts.
- Split ownership clearly: app routes, Jinja templates, CSS authority, JS islands, release/audit scripts, docs.
